# Skills · W · part 42

854 entries, which-security-review to whitespace-expansion. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **which-security-review**: Recommend whether a diff, repository, design, dependency set, or deployment/IAM configuration needs security review, which installed security reviewer or scanner to use, and at what effort. Use when the user asks which security reviewer or scanner to use, whether something needs a security review or threat model, or how deep to go. Direct requests to perform a security review go straight to a reviewer and do not trigger this router. Reports an exact invocation and stops. ([hristokbonev/which-way](https://github.com/hristokbonev/which-way/tree/HEAD/skills/which-security-review))
- **which-shape**: Recommend the right problem-solving loop before choosing individual skills. Reads the explicit shape registry plus project adapter/profile state, then returns an advisory route such as project-intake, bug-fix, concept-rename, or task-closeout (illustrative — shapes.json is the sole shape inventory). Use when the user describes a messy situation and should not need to understand the skill catalog. ([KhurrumMahmood/senior-vibe-engineer](https://github.com/KhurrumMahmood/senior-vibe-engineer/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/which-shape))
- **which-sites-are-most-at-risk**: Ranks a list of places (stores, offices, warehouses, plants, homes) by how much their climate changes later this century, hazard by hazard, in plain words with one chart. Use when someone asks which of several sites, locations or properties is most at risk from climate change, heat, heavy rain or wildfire. ([RiskThinking/crc-docs](https://github.com/RiskThinking/crc-docs/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/which-sites-are-most-at-risk) · ★2)
- **which-skill**: Route a fuzzy request to the right skill in this library. Use when the user is unsure which skill fits, asks 'which skill should I use for X', describes a task without naming a skill, or when a request could plausibly match several skills. Produces a best-fit recommendation with the inputs to gather, a runner-up with the tie-breaker, and a workflow recipe when the job spans multiple skills. ([mohitagw15856/pm-claude-skills](https://github.com/mohitagw15856/pm-claude-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/pm-essentials/skills/which-skill) · ★1.6k · 3 copies)
